## Admission and shared ownership

The current tool environment reports 101 collaboration slots; this documentation owner has two separate reviewer slots plus one architect reserved by the coordinator. That is reported capacity, not demonstrated simultaneous project-chat or build throughput. The initial six implementation chats plus the delivery steward were observed active in isolated app worktrees at `0d54973`, configured gpt-6.1-sol High. The coordinator subsequently admitted T044 baseline preparation: eight project lanes including delivery, with two independent review slots reserved per owner (16 total). Actual memory/CPU/build contention and parallel-chat behavior still require observation; reduce admission when resources demand it. Architect reviews plans, repeated errors and completed long work only.

Coordinator chat: `01a1300d-2ba0-7832-97d0-91d070e2d297`. It alone grants exclusive merge windows. Each owner works on a `codex/` branch in its own worktree; no edits in another checkout. Root Cargo.toml/external dependencies require coordination. Cargo.lock integration is serialized at the merge gate and must preserve other packages. Shared exports are additive, integrated and rechecked. For this checkpoint, as for historical [#222/PR223](https://github.com/anilbeesetti/ktlsp/pull/223), only README.md, docs/HANDOFF.md, docs/PLAN.md, docs/TICKETS.md, docs/status/parity.md and docs/status/development.md are writable. AGENTS/specs/research/product/Cargo/lock/workflows/vendor/other docs remain read-only. Historical #203/PR204 assessed `0a0af012`/535 from its one assigned integration cut `fa26a992`; its seven-file scope included the durable AGENTS merge rule. That assessment and scope are historical. This new child starts at exact assessed `28ff42d`; its later stable integration base, reviewed head/CI/merge and exclusive lease are separate identities, and no main chasing is authorized. Source/publication/CI authorization is not a merge lease.

Native product/build/core analysis/default tests remain Rust-only; T004 stays data-only. Optional user-existing external JDK/build/run/debug tools are permitted under [runtime-boundary.md](../specs/runtime-boundary.md); no bundled Java or JVM semantic offload. This wave runs no Gradle/Maven or fresh JVM oracle. Recorded-oracle replay is native data processing and is distinct from isolated official execution.

#### First full-union Windows path finding

The adversarial full28-file review at019ef361 identified an absolute-path
admission defect: once a Windows drive prefix existed, internal colons such as
`file:///C:/workspace/src:stream` passed as declared directory roots, unlike
the equivalent macro suffix. ROOT traced every lexical/resolve caller and
changed the shared guard to reject colons in all non-prefix path components,
preserving only the already validated native disk prefix. Four public-operation
Windows cases cover absolute/macro and raw/percent-encoded colon spellings.
An incidental error-reason wording pin was removed while retaining its actual
macro-drive replacement refusal. No other path policy, bounds or assertions
were relaxed. The before-fix counterexample is source-backed, not an executed
Windows observation; exact rechecks and original hosted Windows execution
remain required before acceptance. No local Kotlin runtime was admitted.
